###################################################
###################################################
###### Idea Variation 2 GA Idea Groups Army
###################################################
###################################################

###############################################################
#### Select National Idea army_1
###############################################################

iv2_ga_select_national_idea_army_1 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly
	automation_tick_frequency = 1
	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_1 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_1_1 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_1_2 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_1_3 }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_army
			var:iv2_var_progress_points_army >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_not_reached_national_idea_limit
			var:iv2_var_current_national_idea_limit > var:iv2_var_national_idea_current
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_army_1 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_1 
					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_army_1_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_army_1
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_1
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_1
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					add = 1
				}	
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_progress_points_army
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### National Idea Level 2 army_1_1
###############################################################

iv2_ga_select_national_idea_army_1_1 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly
	automation_tick_frequency = 1
	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_innovation_points_for_nat_idea_army
			has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_1
			var:iv2_var_innovation_points_army >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_army_1_1 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_1_1 
					years = -1 
				}
				remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_1
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_innovation_points_army
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_innovation_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
					add = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Deselect National Idea army_1
###############################################################


iv2_ga_deselect_nat_idea_army_1 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = never

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_1
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_1_1
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_1_2
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_1_3
			}		
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = iv2_tt_give_up_nat_idea_army
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_1_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_1_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_1_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_1_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_1_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_1_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_army_1_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_1
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Select National Idea army_2
###############################################################

iv2_ga_select_national_idea_army_2 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly
	automation_tick_frequency = 1
	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_2 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_2_1 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_2_2 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_2_3 }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_army
			var:iv2_var_progress_points_army >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_not_reached_national_idea_limit
			var:iv2_var_current_national_idea_limit > var:iv2_var_national_idea_current
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_army_2 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_2 
					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_army_2_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_army_2
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_2
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_2
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					add = 1
				}	
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_progress_points_army
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly
	automation_tick_frequency = 1
	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_innovation_points_for_nat_idea_army
			has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_3
			var:iv2_var_innovation_points_army >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_army_3_1 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_3_1 
					years = -1 
				}
				remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_3
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_innovation_points_army
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_innovation_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
					add = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Deselect National Idea army_3
###############################################################


iv2_ga_deselect_nat_idea_army_3 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = never

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_3
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_3_1
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_3_2
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_3_3
			}		
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = iv2_tt_give_up_nat_idea_army
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_3_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_3_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_3_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_3_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_3_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_3_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_army_3_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_3
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Select National Idea army_4
###############################################################

iv2_ga_select_national_idea_army_4 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly
	automation_tick_frequency = 1
	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_4 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_4_1 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_4_2 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_4_3 }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_army
			var:iv2_var_progress_points_army >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_not_reached_national_idea_limit
			var:iv2_var_current_national_idea_limit > var:iv2_var_national_idea_current
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_army_4 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_4 
					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_army_4_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_army_4
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_4
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_4
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					add = 1
				}	
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_progress_points_army
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_army
			var:iv2_var_progress_points_army >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army

					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_army_5_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_army_5
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_5
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_5
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					add = 1
				}	
			}
			custom_tooltip = {

					name = iv2_var_progress_points_army
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

					name = iv2_var_innovation_points_army
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_innovation_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
					add = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### National Idea Level 4 army_5_3
###############################################################

iv2_ga_select_national_idea_army_5_3 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly
	automation_tick_frequency = 1
	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_innovation_points_for_nat_idea_army
			has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_5_2
			var:iv2_var_innovation_points_army >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_army_5_3 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_5_3 
					years = -1 
				}
				remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_5_2
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_innovation_points_army
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_innovation_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
					add = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Deselect National Idea army_5
###############################################################


iv2_ga_deselect_nat_idea_army_5 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = never

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_5
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_5_1
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_5_2
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_5_3
			}		
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = iv2_tt_give_up_nat_idea_army
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_5
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_5
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_5_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_5_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_5_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_5_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_5_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_5_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_army_5_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_5
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Select National Idea army_6
###############################################################

iv2_ga_select_national_idea_army_6 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly
	automation_tick_frequency = 1
	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_6 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_6_1 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_6_2 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_6_3 }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_army
			var:iv2_var_progress_points_army >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_not_reached_national_idea_limit
			var:iv2_var_current_national_idea_limit > var:iv2_var_national_idea_current
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_army_6 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_6 
					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_army_6_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_army_6
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_6
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_6
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					add = 1
				}	
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_progress_points_army
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = iv2_tt_give_up_nat_idea_army
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_7
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_7
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_7_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_7_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_7_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_7_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_7_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_7_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_army_7_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_7
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Select National Idea army_8
###############################################################

iv2_ga_select_national_idea_army_8 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly
	automation_tick_frequency = 1
	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_8 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_8_1 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_8_2 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_8_3 }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_army
			var:iv2_var_progress_points_army >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_not_reached_national_idea_limit
			var:iv2_var_current_national_idea_limit > var:iv2_var_national_idea_current
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_army_8 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_8 
					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_army_8_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_army_8
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_8
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_8
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					add = 1
				}	
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_progress_points_army
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = never

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_8

				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_8_2

			}		
		}
	}

					limit = {

						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_8_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_8_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_8_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_8_2

						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase

					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army

	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_10 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_10_1 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_10_2 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_10_3 }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_army
			var:iv2_var_progress_points_army >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_not_reached_national_idea_limit
			var:iv2_var_current_national_idea_limit > var:iv2_var_national_idea_current
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_army_10 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_10 
					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_army_10_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_army_10
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_10
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_10
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					add = 1
				}	
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_progress_points_army
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### National Idea Level 2 army_10_1

iv2_ga_select_national_idea_army_10_1 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly
	automation_tick_frequency = 1
	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_innovation_points_for_nat_idea_army
			has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_10
			var:iv2_var_innovation_points_army >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_army_10_1 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_10_1 
					years = -1 
				}
				remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_10
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_innovation_points_army
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
				}

				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
					add = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}

###############################################################
#### Deselect National Idea army_10
###############################################################


iv2_ga_deselect_nat_idea_army_10 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = never

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_10
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_10_1
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_10_2
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_10_3
			}		
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = iv2_tt_give_up_nat_idea_army
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_10
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_10
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_10_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_10_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_10_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_10_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_10_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_10_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_army_10_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_10
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

			var:iv2_var_innovation_points_army >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_army_12_1 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_12_1 
					years = -1 
				}
				remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_12
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_innovation_points_army
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_innovation_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
					add = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Deselect National Idea army_12
###############################################################


iv2_ga_deselect_nat_idea_army_12 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = never

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_12
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_12_1
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_12_2
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_12_3
			}		
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = iv2_tt_give_up_nat_idea_army
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_12
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_12
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_12_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_12_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_12_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_12_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_12_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_12_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_army_12_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_12
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Select National Idea army_13
###############################################################

iv2_ga_select_national_idea_army_13 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly
	automation_tick_frequency = 1
	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_13 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_13_1 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_13_2 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_13_3 }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_army
			var:iv2_var_progress_points_army >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_not_reached_national_idea_limit
			var:iv2_var_current_national_idea_limit > var:iv2_var_national_idea_current
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_army_13 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_13 
					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_army_13_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_army_13
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_13
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_13
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					add = 1
				}	
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_progress_points_army
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

					name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
					add = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################

	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	allow = {

			has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_14_2
			var:iv2_var_innovation_points_army >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_army_14_3 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_14_3 
					years = -1 
				}
				remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_14_2
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_innovation_points_army
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {

				change_variable = {

###############################################################
#### Deselect National Idea army_14
###############################################################


iv2_ga_deselect_nat_idea_army_14 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = never

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_14
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_14_1
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_14_2
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_14_3
			}		
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = iv2_tt_give_up_nat_idea_army
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_14
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_14
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_14_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_14_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_14_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_14_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_14_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_14_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_army_14_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_14
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Select National Idea army_15
###############################################################

iv2_ga_select_national_idea_army_15 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly
	automation_tick_frequency = 1
	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_15 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_15_1 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_15_2 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_15_3 }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_army
			var:iv2_var_progress_points_army >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_not_reached_national_idea_limit
			var:iv2_var_current_national_idea_limit > var:iv2_var_national_idea_current
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_army_15 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_15 
					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_army_15_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_army_15
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_15
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_15
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					add = 1
				}	
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_progress_points_army
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_16
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_16
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_16_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_16_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_16_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_16_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_16_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_16_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_army_16_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_16
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Select National Idea army_17
###############################################################

iv2_ga_select_national_idea_army_17 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly
	automation_tick_frequency = 1
	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_17 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_17_1 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_17_2 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_17_3 }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_army
			var:iv2_var_progress_points_army >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_not_reached_national_idea_limit
			var:iv2_var_current_national_idea_limit > var:iv2_var_national_idea_current
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_army_17 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_17 
					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_army_17_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_army_17
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_17
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_17
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					add = 1
				}	
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_progress_points_army
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Deselect National Idea army_17
###############################################################


iv2_ga_deselect_nat_idea_army_17 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = never

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_17
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_17_1
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_17_2
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_17_3
			}		
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = iv2_tt_give_up_nat_idea_army
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_17
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_17
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_17_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_17_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_17_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_17_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_17_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_17_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_army_17_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_17
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_19 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_19_1 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_19_2 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_19_3 }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_army
			var:iv2_var_progress_points_army >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_not_reached_national_idea_limit
			var:iv2_var_current_national_idea_limit > var:iv2_var_national_idea_current
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_army_19 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_19 
					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_army_19_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_army_19
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_19
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_19
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					add = 1
				}	
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_progress_points_army
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Deselect National Idea army_19
###############################################################


iv2_ga_deselect_nat_idea_army_19 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = never

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_19
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_19_1
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_19_2
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_19_3
			}		
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = iv2_tt_give_up_nat_idea_army
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_19
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_19
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_19_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_19_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_19_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_19_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_19_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_19_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_army_19_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_19
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

			var:iv2_var_innovation_points_army >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_army_21_1 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_21_1 
					years = -1 
				}
				remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_21
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_innovation_points_army
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_innovation_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
					add = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Deselect National Idea army_21
###############################################################


iv2_ga_deselect_nat_idea_army_21 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = never

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_21
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_21_1
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_21_2
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_21_3
			}		
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = iv2_tt_give_up_nat_idea_army
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_21
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_21
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_21_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_21_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_21_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_21_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_21_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_21_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_army_21_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_21
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Select National Idea army_22
###############################################################

iv2_ga_select_national_idea_army_22 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly
	automation_tick_frequency = 1
	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_22 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_22_1 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_22_2 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_22_3 }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_army
			var:iv2_var_progress_points_army >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_not_reached_national_idea_limit
			var:iv2_var_current_national_idea_limit > var:iv2_var_national_idea_current
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_army_22 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_22 
					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_army_22_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_army_22
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_22
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_22
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					add = 1
				}	
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_progress_points_army
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

					name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
					add = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

			var:iv2_var_innovation_points_army >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army

			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_army_23_3 = yes

			}

			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_army
				change_variable = {

					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
				}

###############################################################
#### Deselect National Idea army_23
###############################################################


iv2_ga_deselect_nat_idea_army_23 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = never

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_23
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_23_1
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_23_2
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_23_3
			}		
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = iv2_tt_give_up_nat_idea_army
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_23
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_23
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_23_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_23_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_23_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_23_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_23_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_23_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_army_23_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_23
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Select National Idea army_24
###############################################################

iv2_ga_select_national_idea_army_24 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly
	automation_tick_frequency = 1
	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_24 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_24_1 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_24_2 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_24_3 }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_army
			var:iv2_var_progress_points_army >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_not_reached_national_idea_limit
			var:iv2_var_current_national_idea_limit > var:iv2_var_national_idea_current
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_army_24 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_24 
					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_army_24_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_army_24
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_24
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_24
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					add = 1
				}	
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_progress_points_army
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_25

					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_25
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_25_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_25_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_25_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_25_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_25_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_25_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_army_25_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_25
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Select National Idea army_26
###############################################################

iv2_ga_select_national_idea_army_26 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly
	automation_tick_frequency = 1
	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_26 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_26_1 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_26_2 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_26_3 }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_army
			var:iv2_var_progress_points_army >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_not_reached_national_idea_limit
			var:iv2_var_current_national_idea_limit > var:iv2_var_national_idea_current
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_army_26 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_26 
					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_army_26_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_army_26
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_26
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_26
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					add = 1
				}	
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_progress_points_army
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Deselect National Idea army_26
###############################################################


iv2_ga_deselect_nat_idea_army_26 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = never

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_26
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_26_1
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_26_2
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_26_3
			}		
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = iv2_tt_give_up_nat_idea_army
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_26
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_26
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_26_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_26_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_26_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_26_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_26_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_26_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_army_26_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_26
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_28 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_28_1 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_28_2 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_28_3 }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_army
			var:iv2_var_progress_points_army >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_not_reached_national_idea_limit
			var:iv2_var_current_national_idea_limit > var:iv2_var_national_idea_current
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_army_28 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_28 
					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_army_28_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_army_28
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_28
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_28
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					add = 1
				}	
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_progress_points_army
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Deselect National Idea army_28
###############################################################


iv2_ga_deselect_nat_idea_army_28 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = never

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_28
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_28_1
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_28_2
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_28_3
			}		
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = iv2_tt_give_up_nat_idea_army
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_28
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_28
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_28_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_28_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_28_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_28_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_28_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_28_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_army_28_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_28
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

			var:iv2_var_innovation_points_army >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_army_30_1 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_30_1 
					years = -1 
				}
				remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_30
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_innovation_points_army
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_innovation_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
					add = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Deselect National Idea army_30
###############################################################


iv2_ga_deselect_nat_idea_army_30 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = never

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_30
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_30_1
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_30_2
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_30_3
			}		
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = iv2_tt_give_up_nat_idea_army
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_30
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_30
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_30_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_30_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_30_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_30_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_30_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_30_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_army_30_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_30
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Select National Idea army_31
###############################################################

iv2_ga_select_national_idea_army_31 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly
	automation_tick_frequency = 1
	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_31 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_31_1 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_31_2 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_31_3 }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_army
			var:iv2_var_progress_points_army >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_not_reached_national_idea_limit
			var:iv2_var_current_national_idea_limit > var:iv2_var_national_idea_current
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_army_31 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_31 
					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_army_31_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_army_31
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_31
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_army_31
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					add = 1
				}	
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_progress_points_army
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_army
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

					name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
					add = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################

			has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_32_2
			var:iv2_var_innovation_points_army >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_army_32_3 = yes
		}

	ai_will_do = {

		}

	effect = {

				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_32_3 
					years = -1 
				}
				remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_army_32_2
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_army
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_innovation_points_army
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_army
				}
